### Y028 kind=run exit=2
```
python3 -c '
with open("internal/server/evaluator.go", "r") as f:
    content = f.read()

target = """func (s *Server) evaluate(ctx context.Context, r *flipt.EvaluationRequest) (*flipt.EvaluationResponse, error) {
	var (
		resp = &flipt.EvaluationResponse{
			RequestId:      r.RequestId,
			EntityId:       r.EntityId,
			RequestContext: r.Context,
			FlagKey:        r.FlagKey,
		}
	)

	flag, err := s.store.GetFlag(ctx, r.FlagKey)
	if err != nil {
		return resp, err
	}

	if !flag.Enabled {
		resp.Match = false
		return resp, nil
	}

	rules, err := s.store.GetEvaluationRules(ctx, r.FlagKey)
	if err != nil {
		return resp, err
	}

	if len(rules) == 0 {
		s.logger.Debug("no rules match")
		return resp, nil
	}

	var lastRank int32

	// rule loop
	for _, rule := range rules {
		if rule.Rank < lastRank {
			return resp, errs.ErrInvalidf("rule rank: %d detected out of order", rule.Rank)
		}

		lastRank = rule.Rank

		constraintMatches := 0

		// constraint loop
		for _, c := range rule.Constraints {
			v := r.Context[c.Property]

			var (
				match bool
				err   error
			)

			switch c.Type {
			case flipt.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE:
				match = matchesString(c, v)
			case flipt.ComparisonType_NUMBER_COMPARISON_TYPE:
				match, err = matchesNumber(c, v)
			case flipt.ComparisonType_BOOLEAN_COMPARISON_TYPE:
				match, err = matchesBool(c, v)
			default:
				return resp, errs.ErrInvalid("unknown constraint type")
			}

			if err != nil {
				return resp, err
			}

			if match {
				s.logger.Debug("constraint matches", zap.Reflect("constraint", c))

				// increase the matchCount
				constraintMatches++

				switch rule.SegmentMatchType {
				case flipt.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:
					// can short circuit here since we had at least one match
					break
				default:
					// keep looping as we need to match all constraints
					continue
				}
			} else {
				// no match
				s.logger.Debug("constraint does not match", zap.Reflect("constraint", c))

				switch rule.SegmentMatchType {
				case flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:
					// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints
					break
				default:
					// keep looping to see if we match the next constraint
					continue
				}
			}
		} // end constraint loop

		switch rule.SegmentMatchType {
		case flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:
			if len(rule.Constraints) != constraintMatches {
				// all constraints did not match, continue to next rule
				s.logger.Debug("did not match ALL constraints")
				continue
			}

		case flipt.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:
			if len(rule.Constraints) > 0 && constraintMatches == 0 {
				// no constraints matched, continue to next rule
				s.logger.Debug("did not match ANY constraints")
				continue
			}
		default:
			s.logger.Error("unknown match type", zap.Int32("match_type", int32(rule.SegmentMatchType)))
			continue
		}

		// otherwise, this is our matching rule, determine the flag variant to return
		// based on the distributions
		resp.SegmentKey = rule.SegmentKey

		distributions, err := s.store.GetEvaluationDistributions(ctx, rule.ID)
		if err != nil {
			return resp, err
		}

		var (
			validDistributions []*storage.EvaluationDistribution
			buckets            []int
		)

		for _, d := range distributions {
			// don't include 0% rollouts
			if d.Rollout > 0 {
				validDistributions = append(validDistributions, d)

				if buckets == nil {
					bucket := int(d.Rollout * percentMultiplier)
					buckets = append(buckets, bucket)
				} else {
					bucket := buckets[len(buckets)-1] + int(d.Rollout*percentMultiplier)
					buckets = append(buckets, bucket)
				}
			}
		}

		// no distributions for rule
		if len(validDistributions) == 0 {
			s.logger.Info("no distributions for rule")
			resp.Match = true
			return resp, nil
		}

		var (
			bucket = crc32Num(r.EntityId, r.FlagKey)
			// sort.SearchInts searches for x in a sorted slice of ints and returns the index
			// as specified by Search. The return value is the index to insert x if x is
			// not present (it could be len(a)).
			index = sort.SearchInts(buckets, int(bucket)+1)
		)

		// if index is outside of our existing buckets then it does not match any distribution
		if index == len(validDistributions) {
			resp.Match = false
			s.logger.Debug("did not match any distributions")
			return resp, nil
		}

		d := validDistributions[index]
		s.logger.Debug("matched distribution", zap.Reflect("evaluation_distribution", d))

		resp.Match = true
		resp.Value = d.VariantKey
		resp.Attachment = d.VariantAttachment
		return resp, nil
	} // end rule loop

	return resp, nil
}"""

replacement = """func (s *Server) evaluate(ctx context.Context, r *flipt.EvaluationRequest) (*flipt.EvaluationResponse, error) {
	var (
		resp = &flipt.EvaluationResponse{
			RequestId:      r.RequestId,
			EntityId:       r.EntityId,
			RequestContext: r.Context,
			FlagKey:        r.FlagKey,
			Reason:         flipt.EvaluationReason_UNKNOWN_EVALUATION_REASON,
		}
	)

	flag, err := s.store.GetFlag(ctx, r.FlagKey)
	if err != nil {
		var errnf errs.ErrNotFound
		if errors.As(err, &errnf) {
			resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_FLAG_NOT_FOUND_EVALUATION_REASON
		} else {
			resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON
		}
		return resp, err
	}

	if !flag.Enabled {
		resp.Match = false
		resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_FLAG_DISABLED_EVALUATION_REASON
		return resp, nil
	}

	rules, err := s.store.GetEvaluationRules(ctx, r.FlagKey)
	if err != nil {
		resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON
		return resp, err
	}

	if len(rules) == 0 {
		s.logger.Debug("no rules match")
		return resp, nil
	}

	var lastRank int32

	// rule loop
	for _, rule := range rules {
		if rule.Rank < lastRank {
			resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON
			return resp, errs.ErrInvalidf("rule rank: %d detected out of order", rule.Rank)
		}

		lastRank = rule.Rank

		constraintMatches := 0

		// constraint loop
		for _, c := range rule.Constraints {
			v := r.Context[c.Property]

			var (
				match bool
				err   error
			)

			switch c.Type {
			case flipt.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE:
				match = matchesString(c, v)
			case flipt.ComparisonType_NUMBER_COMPARISON_TYPE:
				match, err = matchesNumber(c, v)
			case flipt.ComparisonType_BOOLEAN_COMPARISON_TYPE:
				match, err = matchesBool(c, v)
			default:
				resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON
				return resp, errs.ErrInvalid("unknown constraint type")
			}

			if err != nil {
				resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON
				return resp, err
			}

			if match {
				s.logger.Debug("constraint matches", zap.Reflect("constraint", c))

				// increase the matchCount
				constraintMatches++

				switch rule.SegmentMatchType {
				case flipt.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:
					// can short circuit here since we had at least one match
					break
				default:
					// keep looping as we need to match all constraints
					continue
				}
			} else {
				// no match
				s.logger.Debug("constraint does not match", zap.Reflect("constraint", c))

				switch rule.SegmentMatchType {
				case flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:
					// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints
					break
				default:
					// keep looping to see if we match the next constraint
					continue
				}
			}
		} // end constraint loop

		switch rule.SegmentMatchType {
		case flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:
			if len(rule.Constraints) != constraintMatches {
				// all constraints did not match, continue to next rule
				s.logger.Debug("did not match ALL constraints")
				continue
			}

		case flipt.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:
			if len(rule.Constraints) > 0 && constraintMatches == 0 {
				// no constraints matched, continue to next rule
				s.logger.Debug("did not match ANY constraints")
				continue
			}
		default:
			s.logger.Error("unknown match type", zap.Int32("match_type", int32(rule.SegmentMatchType)))
			continue
		}

		// otherwise, this is our matching rule, determine the flag variant to return
		// based on the distributions
		resp.SegmentKey = rule.SegmentKey

		distributions, err := s.store.GetEvaluationDistributions(ctx, rule.ID)
		if err != nil {
			resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON
			return resp, err
		}

		var (
			validDistributions []*storage.EvaluationDistribution
			buckets            []int
		)

		for _, d := range distributions {
			// don't include 0% rollouts
			if d.Rollout > 0 {
				validDistributions = append(validDistributions, d)

				if buckets == nil {
					bucket := int(d.Rollout * percentMultiplier)
					buckets = append(buckets, bucket)
				} else {
					bucket := buckets[len(buckets)-1] + int(d.Rollout*percentMultiplier)
					buckets = append(buckets, bucket)
				}
			}
		}

		// no distributions for rule
		if len(validDistributions) == 0 {
			s.logger.Info("no distributions for rule")
			resp.Match = true
			resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON
			return resp, nil
		}

		var (
			bucket = crc32Num(r.EntityId, r.FlagKey)
			// sort.SearchInts searches for x in a sorted slice of ints and returns the index
			// as specified by Search. The return value is the index to insert x if x is
			// not present (it could be len(a)).
			index = sort.SearchInts(buckets, int(bucket)+1)
		)

		// if index is outside of our existing buckets then it does not match any distribution
		if index == len(validDistributions) {
			resp.Match = false
			s.logger.Debug("did not match any distributions")
			return resp, nil
		}

		d := validDistributions[index]
		s.logger.Debug("matched distribution", zap.Reflect("evaluation_distribution", d))

		resp.Match = true
		resp.Reason = flipt.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON
		resp.Value = d.VariantKey
		resp.Attachment = d.VariantAttachment
		return resp, nil
	} // end rule loop

	return resp, nil
}"""
```

```
cat << 'EOF' > internal/server/reason_test.go
package server

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	errs "go.flipt.io/flipt/errors"
	"go.flipt.io/flipt/internal/storage"
	flipt "go.flipt.io/flipt/rpc/flipt"
	"go.uber.org/zap/zaptest"
)

func TestEvaluationReason_AllScenarios(t *testing.T) {
	logger := zaptest.NewLogger(t)

	t.Run("FlagNotFound", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "missing").Return(&flipt.Flag{}, errs.ErrNotFoundf("flag %q", "missing"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "missing",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_FLAG_NOT_FOUND_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("FlagDisabled", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "disabled").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "disabled", Enabled: false}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "disabled",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_FLAG_DISABLED_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("NoRules_UnknownReason", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "norules").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "norules", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "norules").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "norules",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_UNKNOWN_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("RuleMatched_DistributionMatched", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "matched").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "matched", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "matched").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				FlagKey:          "matched",
				SegmentKey:       "s1",
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Rank:             1,
				Constraints: []storage.EvaluationConstraint{
					{
						ID:       "c1",
						Type:     flipt.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE,
						Property: "env",
						Operator: "eq",
						Value:    "prod",
					},
				},
			},
		}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution{
			{
				ID:                "d1",
				RuleID:            "r1",
				VariantKey:        "v1",
				VariantAttachment: "att1",
				Rollout:           100.0,
			},
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "matched",
			EntityId: "user1",
```

```
func TestEvaluationReason_AllScenarios(t *testing.T) {
	logger := zaptest.NewLogger(t)

	t.Run("FlagNotFound", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "missing").Return(&flipt.Flag{}, errs.ErrNotFoundf("flag %q", "missing"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "missing",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_FLAG_NOT_FOUND_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("FlagDisabled", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "disabled").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "disabled", Enabled: false}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "disabled",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_FLAG_DISABLED_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("NoRules_UnknownReason", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "norules").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "norules", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "norules").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "norules",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_UNKNOWN_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("RuleMatched_DistributionMatched", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}

		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "matched").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "matched", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "matched").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				FlagKey:          "matched",
				SegmentKey:       "s1",
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Rank:             1,
				Constraints: []storage.EvaluationConstraint{
					{
						ID:       "c1",
						Type:     flipt.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE,
						Property: "env",
						Operator: "eq",
						Value:    "prod",
					},
				},
			},
		}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution{
			{
				ID:                "d1",
				RuleID:            "r1",
				VariantKey:        "v1",
				VariantAttachment: "att1",
				Rollout:           100.0,
			},
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "matched",
			EntityId: "user1",
			Context:  map[string]string{"env": "prod"},
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.True(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, "v1", resp.Value)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("RuleMatched_NoDistributions", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "nodist").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "nodist", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "nodist").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				FlagKey:          "nodist",
				SegmentKey:       "s1",
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Rank:             1,
			},
		}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution{}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "nodist",
			EntityId: "user1",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)

	})

	t.Run("RuleMatched_OutsideRollout_UnknownReason", func(t *testing.T) {

		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "rollout").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				FlagKey:          "rollout",
				SegmentKey:       "s1",
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Rank:             1,
			},
		}, nil)
		// 0% rollout or very small rollout where entity does not match
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution{
			{
				ID:         "d1",
				RuleID:     "r1",
				VariantKey: "v1",
				Rollout:    0.0001, // entity with crc32 bucket > 0 won't match
			},
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "rollout",
			EntityId: "random_entity_9999",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_UNKNOWN_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_GetFlag", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "dberr").Return(&flipt.Flag{}, errors.New("db connection failure"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "dberr",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_GetEvaluationRules", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "ruleserr").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "ruleserr", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "ruleserr").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule(nil), errors.New("rules db error"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "ruleserr",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_RulesOutOfOrder", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "outoforder").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "outoforder", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "outoforder").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{ID: "r1", Rank: 2},
			{ID: "r2", Rank: 1}, // rank 1 < rank 2
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "outoforder",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)

	})

	t.Run("Error_ConstraintEvaluation", func(t *testing.T) {

		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "constrainterr").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "constrainterr", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "constrainterr").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{

					{
						ID:       "c1",

						Operator: "eq",
						Value:    "not_a_number",

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
```

```
	t.Run("RuleMatched_NoDistributions", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "nodist").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "nodist", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "nodist").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				FlagKey:          "nodist",
				SegmentKey:       "s1",
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Rank:             1,
			},
		}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution{}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "nodist",
			EntityId: "user1",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.True(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("RuleMatched_OutsideRollout_UnknownReason", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "rollout").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "rollout", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "rollout").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				FlagKey:          "rollout",
				SegmentKey:       "s1",
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Rank:             1,
			},
		}, nil)
		// 0% rollout or very small rollout where entity does not match
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution{
			{
				ID:         "d1",
				RuleID:     "r1",
				VariantKey: "v1",
				Rollout:    0.0001, // entity with crc32 bucket > 0 won't match
			},
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "rollout",
			EntityId: "random_entity_9999",
		})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, resp.Match)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_UNKNOWN_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_GetFlag", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "dberr").Return(&flipt.Flag{}, errors.New("db connection failure"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "dberr",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_GetEvaluationRules", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "ruleserr").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "ruleserr", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "ruleserr").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule(nil), errors.New("rules db error"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "ruleserr",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_RulesOutOfOrder", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "outoforder").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "outoforder", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "outoforder").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				Rank:             2,
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Constraints: []storage.EvaluationConstraint{
					{
						ID:       "c1",
						Type:     flipt.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE,
						Property: "env",
						Operator: "eq",
						Value:    "stage",
					},
				},
			},
			{ID: "r2", Rank: 1}, // rank 1 < rank 2
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "outoforder",
			EntityId: "1",
			Context:  map[string]string{"env": "prod"}, // won't match r1
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_ConstraintEvaluation", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "constrainterr").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "constrainterr", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "constrainterr").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				Rank:             1,
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
				Constraints: []storage.EvaluationConstraint{
					{
						ID:       "c1",
						Type:     flipt.ComparisonType_NUMBER_COMPARISON_TYPE,
						Property: "age",
						Operator: "eq",
						Value:    "not_a_number",
					},
				},
			},
		}, nil)

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "constrainterr",
			EntityId: "1",
			Context:  map[string]string{"age": "25"},
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})

	t.Run("Error_GetEvaluationDistributions", func(t *testing.T) {
		store := &storeMock{}
		s := &Server{logger: logger, store: store}
		store.On("GetFlag", mock.Anything, "disterr").Return(&flipt.Flag{Key: "disterr", Enabled: true}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationRules", mock.Anything, "disterr").Return([]*storage.EvaluationRule{
			{
				ID:               "r1",
				Rank:             1,
				SegmentMatchType: flipt.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE,
			},
		}, nil)
		store.On("GetEvaluationDistributions", mock.Anything, "r1").Return([]*storage.EvaluationDistribution(nil), errors.New("distribution db error"))

		resp, err := s.Evaluate(context.Background(), &flipt.EvaluationRequest{
			FlagKey:  "disterr",
			EntityId: "1",
		})
		require.Error(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, flipt.EvaluationReason_ERROR_EVALUATION_REASON, resp.Reason)
	})
}
```
